Skip to chat input

TealClaw

BYOK voice AI dashboard. Zero server. Total privacy. 110+ configurable fields.

TealClaw is a Progressive Web App that runs entirely in your browser. Your API keys never leave your device. All API calls go directly from your browser to the AI provider.

Features

  • Multi-turn chat with streaming and typing animations
  • Voice input (Groq Whisper) and output (Groq Orpheus TTS)
  • Image generation (/imagine) and vision (camera, drag-drop, paste)
  • Deep research reports (/research) with segmented results
  • 12 built-in style templates (/template)
  • Custom bot persona -- name, icon, greeting, system prompt
  • Full visual customization -- colors, fonts, backgrounds, animations
  • Multi-conversation with IndexedDB, encrypted export/import
  • Config profiles, QR code sharing, Telegram forwarding
  • Multi-model routing, LaTeX rendering, GIF reactions, rate limiter
  • PIN code security, accessibility features, PWA installable

For AI Agents

The complete agent skill guide is at tealclaw.ai/llms.txt. Any AI agent can read that file to learn how to configure TealClaw via JSON. A single JSON config block in a chat response auto-applies all settings in real time.

Config delivery methods: inline JSON (auto-applied), config file (drag-drop), or URL hash (tealclaw.ai/#config=BASE64).

Source code and skill files are on GitHub at github.com/Snail3D/tealclaw. The SKILL.md file contains the full agent skill definition with config schema, delivery methods, recipes, and troubleshooting.

Links

  • Agent Skill Guide (llms.txt)
  • GitHub Repository
  • SKILL.md (agent skill definition)

TealClaw

Created by Snail
Free, open-source, and provably anonymous forever.

TealClaw started as a simple idea: what if you could have a fully customizable AI chat interface that runs entirely in your browser, with no server, no accounts, and no data collection?

Every feature — from voice I/O to streaming, templates, GIF reactions, interactive components, and 110+ config fields — was built with one goal: give you complete control over your AI experience.

Your API keys never leave your device. Your conversations stay in your browser. TealClaw is and always will be free to use.

More than a chatbot

Connect to OpenClaw and TealClaw becomes a full control surface — your AI can manage smart devices, run automations, monitor cameras, stream video, and execute real-world actions through the tc-action protocol. Create guest links for family. Deep research with live sources. It's an AI dashboard that does whatever you build it to do.

I don't know how to put this succinctly, but if you know the value of what TealClaw is, you'll see that it's a bargain.

The deal: TealClaw remains free, open source, and as anonymous as possible — forever. But right here, in this little heart menu, you can put whatever you want (if it's Godly). That's it. Reach out to Eric Woodard on LinkedIn.

`; } // ── Haptic Feedback ──────────────────────────────────── function haptic(pattern='light'){ const k=getK(); if(k.hapticFeedback===false)return; // default ON if(!navigator.vibrate)return; switch(pattern){ case 'light':navigator.vibrate(8);break; case 'send':navigator.vibrate([5,20,12]);break; case 'receive':navigator.vibrate([8,50,5,30,8]);break; case 'shutter':navigator.vibrate([20,10,5]);break; default:navigator.vibrate(10); } } // Add haptic to all button/interactive taps globally document.addEventListener('click',e=>{ const el=e.target.closest('button,.sheet-row,.mode-btn,.icon-btn,.accent-swatch,.a11y-toggle,.exp-toggle'); }); } // ── GIF Overlay ────────────────────────────────────── let gifTimer=null; let gifFadeTimer=null; async function searchGif(query){ if(!query||getK().gifEnabled===false)return null; try{ const q=encodeURIComponent(String(query).slice(0,80)); const res=await fetch(`/api/gif?q=${q}`); if(!res.ok)return null; const d=await res.json(); if(d.ad)return{ad:d.ad}; return d.url?{url:d.url}:null; }catch{return null} } function parseGifTag(text){ if(!text)return{cleanText:'',gifQuery:null}; // Accept [gif:query] and tolerate missing closing bracket ([gif:query) const m=text.match(/\[gif:\s*([^\]\n]+)\]?/i); if(m){ const gifQuery=(m[1]||'').trim().slice(0,80); const cleanText=text.replace(m[0],'').replace(/[^\S\n]{2,}/g,' ').replace(/\n{3,}/g,'\n\n').trim(); return{cleanText,gifQuery:gifQuery||null}; } return{cleanText:text,gifQuery:null}; } // Auto-detect GIF-worthy responses when the AI doesn't include [gif:] tags function autoDetectGif(text){ if(!text||text.length<10||text.length>600)return null; const t=text.toLowerCase(); // Greetings if(/^(hey|hello|hi|howdy|what'?s up|yo|greetings)\b/i.test(t))return 'hello wave'; // Celebrations / success if(/\b(congrat|awesome|amazing|fantastic|great job|well done|nailed it|perfect|bravo|woohoo|yay|let'?s go)\b/i.test(t))return 'celebration'; // Humor / jokes if(/\b(haha|lol|lmao|joke|funny|punchline|knock knock|😂|🤣)\b/i.test(t))return 'laughing'; // Thank you if(/\b(you'?re welcome|no problem|glad to help|happy to help|anytime)\b/i.test(t))return 'thumbs up'; // Mind-blowing if(/\b(mind.?blow|incredible|unbelievable|insane|wow|whoa|no way)\b/i.test(t))return 'mind blown'; // Goodbye if(/\b(goodbye|bye|see you|take care|catch you later|peace out)\b/i.test(t))return 'bye wave'; return null; } const GIF_BASE_MIN_W=100,GIF_BASE_MAX_W=500,GIF_BASE_DEFAULT_W=180; function clampGifScale(val){ const n=Number(val); if(!Number.isFinite(n))return 1; return Math.max(0.5,Math.min(2,n)); } function getGifScale(){ return clampGifScale(getK().gifScale); } function getGifSizeLimits(){ const scale=getGifScale(); return{ scale, min:Math.round(GIF_BASE_MIN_W*scale), max:Math.round(GIF_BASE_MAX_W*scale), defaultW:Math.round(GIF_BASE_DEFAULT_W*scale) }; The user will ask you to research a topic or analyze an image. Return your response as valid JSON with this exact structure: { "verdict": {"label":"SHORT VERDICT","color":"green|yellow|orange|red|gray|blue"}, "summary": "2-3 sentence overview", "sections": [ {"title":"SECTION NAME","content":"Detailed findings. Use **bold** for emphasis.","severity":"neutral|ok|warn|danger","bullets":["point 1","point 2"]}, ... ], "sources": ["source description or URL"], "fullContext": "Extended detailed analysis with all findings, data points, nuances, caveats, and deep context. This should be 3-4x longer than what the user sees. Include raw data, statistical details, alternative interpretations, related tangents, methodology notes, and anything an expert would want to know." } Rules: - Always include 4-8 sections with meaningful titles - Severity: "ok" for positive findings, "warn" for caution, "danger" for critical issues, "neutral" for informational - Verdict colors: green=good, yellow=caution, orange=concerning, red=bad, blue=informational, gray=unknown - The "fullContext" field MUST be 3-4x more detailed than the visible sections combined - Use **markdown bold** in content for emphasis - If analyzing an image, describe what you see in detail - Research thoroughly: cover pros, cons, risks, alternatives, history, and recommendations - Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences or extra text`+TC_CORE_POLICY; function renderResearchHTML(data){ let h='

} // ── Obsidian Auto-Save ───────────────────────────── // Desktop (Chrome/Edge): File System Access API — silent background writes // Mobile / other: obsidian:// URI scheme — opens Obsidian app on flush const ObsidianSync={ _handle:null,_timer:null,_lastLen:0,_dbName:'tealclaw-obsidian', get hasFileAccess(){return typeof window.showDirectoryPicker==='function'}, // ── IDB handle storage (File System Access only) ── async _openHandleDB(){ return new Promise((res,rej)=>{ const req=indexedDB.open(this._dbName,1); '+html+'
','text/html'); const root=doc.body.firstChild; function walk(node){ if(node.nodeType===3)return node.textContent; if(node.nodeType!==1)return ''; const tag=node.tagName.toLowerCase(); const kids=Array.from(node.childNodes).map(walk).join(''); switch(tag){ case 'h1': return '\n# '+kids.trim()+'\n'; case 'h2': return '\n## '+kids.trim()+'\n'; case 'h3': return '\n### '+kids.trim()+'\n'; case 'h4': return '\n#### '+kids.trim()+'\n'; case 'strong':case 'b': return '**'+kids+'**'; case 'em':case 'i': return '*'+kids+'*'; case 'code': return node.parentElement?.tagName==='PRE'?kids:'`'+kids+'`'; case 'pre': {const lang=node.dataset?.lang||'';return '\n```'+lang+'\n'+kids.trim()+'\n```\n'} case 'a': {const href=node.getAttribute('href');return href?'['+kids+']('+href+')':kids} case 'br': return '\n'; case 'hr': return '\n---\n'; case 'p': return '\n'+kids.trim()+'\n'; case 'li': return '- '+kids.trim()+'\n'; case 'ul':case 'ol': return '\n'+kids; case 'blockquote': return '\n> '+kids.trim().replace(/\n/g,'\n> ')+'\n'; case 'table': return '\n'+tableToMd(node)+'\n'; case 'img': return '![image]('+node.getAttribute('src')+')'; case 'div': { // Research-specific classes if(node.classList.contains('research-result'))return kids; if(node.classList.contains('research-verdict-green')||node.classList.contains('research-verdict-blue')||node.classList.contains('research-verdict-yellow')||node.classList.contains('research-verdict-orange')||node.classList.contains('research-verdict-red'))return '\n**'+kids.trim()+'**\n'; if(node.classList.contains('research-section'))return kids; if(node.classList.contains('research-section-title'))return '\n## '+kids.trim()+'\n'; if(node.classList.contains('research-sources'))return '\n## Sources\n'+kids; if(node.classList.contains('md-content'))return kids; if(node.classList.contains('research-processing'))return ''; if(node.classList.contains('config-apply-card'))return ''; if(node.classList.contains('tc-action-badge'))return ''; return kids; } case 'svg':case 'button':case 'canvas': return ''; default: return kids; } } function tableToMd(table){ const rows=Array.from(table.querySelectorAll('tr')); if(!rows.length)return ''; let md=''; rows.forEach((row,i)=>{ const cells=Array.from(row.querySelectorAll('th,td')).map(c=>c.textContent.trim()); md+='| '+cells.join(' | ')+' |\n'; if(i===0)md+='| '+cells.map(()=>'---').join(' | ')+' |\n'; }); return md; } return walk(root).replace(/\n{3,}/g,'\n\n').trim(); } function getShareText(idx){ const m=chatHistory[idx];if(!m)return ''; // Raw HTML messages (research results, system bubbles) → convert to markdown if(m.raw)return htmlToMd(m.text||''); `); } } // ── Portable JSON Export/Import ───────────────────── const SENSITIVE_KEYS = new Set(['aiKey','whisperKey','tgToken','tgChatId','imageGenKey','pinHash','pinRequired']); function compressImageDataUrl(dataUrl){ if(!dataUrl||!dataUrl.startsWith('data:image'))return Promise.resolve(dataUrl); if(!m)return null; const n=parseInt(m[1]); const unit=m[2].toLowerCase(); const mult={m:60000,h:3600000,d:86400000,w:604800000}; return n*(mult[unit]||86400000); } function formatTimeLeft(ms){ if(ms<=0)return 'expired'; const mins=Math.floor(ms/60000); if(mins<60)return `${mins}m`; const hrs=Math.floor(mins/60); if(hrs<24)return `${hrs}h ${mins%60}m`; const days=Math.floor(hrs/24); return `${days}d ${hrs%24}h`; } function checkSessionExpiry(){ const s=getSession(); }catch(e){console.warn('Webhook failed:',e)} } // ── Multi-Model Routing ───────────────────────────── function routeModel(text,k){ if(!k.fastModel&&!k.complexModel)return null; const threshold=+(k.routingThreshold||80); const complexPatterns=/\b(explain|analyze|compare|research|write|create|design|implement|debug|review|summarize|translate|elaborate|detail|comprehensive|thorough)\b/i; const isComplex=text.length>threshold||complexPatterns.test(text); return isComplex?(k.complexModel||null):(k.fastModel||null); } // ── Auto-Config Detection (in AI responses) ─────── const TC_ACTION_TYPES=new Set(['config','command','save','research','deepresearch','imagine','toast','style','navigate','bubble','video','video-grid','gallery','surveillance','guest-link','prompt','request-config','file','alarm','timer','cron','pomodoro','debate','camera-capture','camera.capture','screen-capture','screen.capture']); // ── Agent Action Protocol (tc-action) ────────────── // Agents embed ```tc-action blocks in responses to control TealClaw. // Parsed, executed, and stripped before display. function parseTcActions(text){ const actions=[]; const stripBlocks=[]; const re=/```tc-action\s*([\s\S]*?)```/g; let m; while((m=re.exec(text))!==null){ try{ const obj=JSON.parse(m[1].trim()); if(obj&&typeof obj==='object'){ const t=(typeof obj.type==='string'&&obj.type)||''; const k=(typeof obj.kind==='string'&&obj.kind)||''; if(k==='camera.capture'&&!t)obj.type='camera-capture'; if(k==='screen.capture'&&!t)obj.type='screen-capture'; if(obj.type==='camera.capture')obj.type='camera-capture'; if(obj.type==='screen.capture')obj.type='screen-capture'; if(typeof obj.type==='string'&&TC_ACTION_TYPES.has(obj.type)){ actions.push(obj); stripBlocks.push(m[0]); } } }catch(e){console.warn('tc-action parse failed:',e.message,m[1].trim().slice(0,200))} } // Help mode fallback: some models ignore the tc-action fence and emit plain JSON. if(_helpMode&&!actions.length){ const altRe=/```(?:json|js|javascript)\s*([\s\S]*?)```/gi; let a; while((a=altRe.exec(text))!==null){ try{ const obj=JSON.parse(a[1].trim()); if(obj&&typeof obj==='object'){ const t=(typeof obj.type==='string'&&obj.type)||''; const k=(typeof obj.kind==='string'&&obj.kind)||''; if(k==='camera.capture'&&!t)obj.type='camera-capture'; if(k==='screen.capture'&&!t)obj.type='screen-capture'; if(obj.type==='camera.capture')obj.type='camera-capture'; if(obj.type==='screen.capture')obj.type='screen-capture'; if(typeof obj.type==='string'&&TC_ACTION_TYPES.has(obj.type)){ actions.push(obj); stripBlocks.push(a[0]); } } }catch{} } } let cleanText=text; stripBlocks.forEach(block=>{cleanText=cleanText.replace(block,'')}); cleanText=cleanText.trim(); return{cleanText,actions}; } const TC_BLOCKED_CONFIG_KEYS=new Set(['aiKey','whisperKey','aiBaseUrl','aiProvider','directFallbackToGroq','gatewayHttpFallback','gwToken','gwUrl','mode','tgRelayEnabled','pinCode','pinHash','imageGenKey','webhookUrl','webhookEvents','customCSS']); sysBubble(`

${esc(title)}

${esc(details)}

`); const scroll=$('chatScroll'); if(scroll){ if(typeof snapScrollToBottom==='function')snapScrollToBottom(scroll); else scroll.scrollTop=scroll.scrollHeight; } const last=document.querySelector('.chat-bubble:last-child'); const disable=()=>{last?.querySelectorAll('.btn').forEach(b=>{b.style.opacity='0.5';b.style.pointerEvents='none'})}; last?.querySelector('#'+id+'-deny')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{disable();showToast('Action denied','ok');haptic('light');resolve(false)}); last?.querySelector('#'+id+'-allow')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{disable();resolve(true)}); }); } function requestInlineCameraConsent({facing='environment',autoSend=true}={}){ return new Promise(resolve=>{ const id='tc-cam-'+Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(4)),b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join(''); const facingLabel=facing==='both'?'front + back':(facing==='user'?'front':'back'); sysBubble(`

`); const scroll=$('chatScroll'); if(scroll){ if(typeof snapScrollToBottom==='function')snapScrollToBottom(scroll); else scroll.scrollTop=scroll.scrollHeight; } const last=document.querySelector('.chat-bubble:last-child'); last?.querySelector('#'+id+'-take')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{disable();resolve(true)}); }); } // Strip dangerous HTML from agent bubble content function sanitizeActionHtml(html){ const div=document.createElement('div');div.innerHTML=html; div.querySelectorAll('script,iframe,object,embed,form,meta,link,base,svg,math,style').forEach(el=>el.remove()); div.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(el=>{ for(const attr of[...el.attributes]){ const name=attr.name.toLowerCase(); if(name.startsWith('on')){el.removeAttribute(attr.name);continue} const val=(attr.value||'').trim().toLowerCase(); if(val.startsWith('javascript:')||val.startsWith('data:text/html')||val.startsWith('vbscript:')){el.removeAttribute(attr.name);continue} } // Strip dangerous CSS from style attributes if(el.hasAttribute('style')){ let s=el.getAttribute('style'); s=s.replace(/url\s*\(/gi,'/* blocked */'); s=s.replace(/expression\s*\(/gi,'/* blocked */'); s=s.replace(/-moz-binding/gi,'/* blocked */'); el.setAttribute('style',s); } }); return div.innerHTML; } async function executeTcActions(actions){ const results=[]; for(const action of actions){ try{ switch(action.type){ case 'config':{ const data=action.data||action; // Resolve CSS named colors (e.g. "pink") to hex for color fields const colorFields=['accentColor','bgColor','textColor','chatUserColor','chatAiColor','chatUserTextColor','chatAiTextColor','topbarBg','inputBarBg','sendBtnColor','micBtnColor','borderColor']; for(const cf of colorFields){ if(data[cf]&&typeof data[cf]==='string'&&!/^#/.test(data[cf])){ const tmp=document.createElement('div');tmp.style.color=data[cf];document.body.appendChild(tmp); const rgb=getComputedStyle(tmp).color;document.body.removeChild(tmp); const m=rgb.match(/(\d+)/g); if(m&&m.length>=3)data[cf]='#'+m.slice(0,3).map(n=>(+n).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join(''); } } const blockedKeys=Object.keys(data).filter(k=>k!=='type'&&TC_BLOCKED_CONFIG_KEYS.has(k)); if(blockedKeys.length){ blockedKeys.forEach(k=>delete data[k]); showToast('Blocked sensitive setting changes: '+blockedKeys.join(', '),'err'); } const keys=Object.keys(data).filter(k=>k!=='type'); if(!keys.length){results.push({type:'config',ok:false,error:'no allowed config fields'});break} const hasSensitive=['aiKey','whisperKey','tgToken','webhookUrl','customCSS','pinHash'].some(s=>data[s]); const desc=keys.slice(0,5).join(', ')+(keys.length>5?'...':'')+(hasSensitive?' const id='mermaid-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,9); const{svg}=await window.mermaid.render(id,src); // Parse SVG safely — strip any event handlers that survived mermaid strict mode const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(svg,'image/svg+xml'); const svgEl=doc.documentElement; svgEl.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(n=>{for(const a of[...n.attributes])if(a.name.toLowerCase().startsWith('on'))n.removeAttribute(a.name)}); block.textContent='';block.appendChild(document.importNode(svgEl,true)); block.classList.add('rendered'); }catch(e){ block.innerHTML=`
${esc(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(block.dataset.mermaid))))}
Diagram error: ${esc(e.message||'Parse failed')}
`; block.classList.add('rendered'); } } } // ── Streaming AI Call ──────────────────────────────── async function callAIStream(userText,imageBase64,onToken){ if(bleGateBlocking())throw new Error('BLE beacon out of range — messaging locked until beacon reconnects.'); const k=getK(); if(k.mode==='agent'&&getActiveAgent())return callAgent(userText,imageBase64,onToken); const provider=resolveAiProvider(k); if(!hasDirectProvider(provider,k)&&!(provider!=='groq'&&hasDirectProvider('groq',k)))return {text:'__NO_KEY__',usage:null}; const ctrl=new AbortController(); window._tcAbort=ctrl; try{ const res=await callDirectAI(userText,imageBase64,{stream:true,onToken,signal:ctrl.signal}); return res; } finally { window._tcActiveReader=null; if(window._tcAbort===ctrl)window._tcAbort=null; } } // ── Scheduled Messages ────────────────────────────── function checkScheduledMessages(){ const k=getK(); if(!k.scheduledMessages||!Array.isArray(k.scheduledMessages))return; const now=new Date();const hhmm=String(now.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0'); const day=['sun','mon','tue','wed','thu','fri','sat'][now.getDay()]; k.scheduledMessages.forEach(msg=>{ if(!msg.time||!msg.text)return; if(msg.time!==hhmm)return; if(msg.days&&!msg.days.includes(day))return; const lastKey='tc-sched-'+msg.time; const lastRun=localStorage.getItem(lastKey); const today=now.toDateString(); if(lastRun===today)return; localStorage.setItem(lastKey,today); setTimeout(()=>{sysBubble(`
${renderMd(msg.text)}
`);haptic('receive')},500); }); } // ── Cron Jobs ────────────────────────────────────── function getCronJobs(){return getK().cronJobs||[]} function setCronJobs(jobs){const k=getK();k.cronJobs=jobs;setK(k)} function addCronJob(job){ const jobs=getCronJobs(); return job; } function updateCronJob(id,updates){ const jobs=getCronJobs(); const job=jobs.find(j=>j.id===id); if(!job)return; Object.assign(job,updates); setCronJobs(jobs); renderCronBar(); } function deleteCronJob(id){ setCronJobs(getCronJobs().filter(j=>j.id!==id)); renderCronBar(); } // ── Alarms ────────────────────────────────────────── function getAlarms(){return getK().alarms||[]} function setAlarms(arr){const k=getK();k.alarms=arr;setK(k)} function addAlarm(alarm){ const arr=getAlarms(); alarm.id=Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6); alarm.createdAt=new Date().toISOString(); if(!alarm.source)alarm.source='local'; arr.push(alarm); setAlarms(arr); renderCronBar(); return alarm; } function removeAlarm(id){ setAlarms(getAlarms().filter(a=>a.id!==id)); renderCronBar(); } // ── Timers ────────────────────────────────────────── function getTimers(){return getK().timers||[]} function setTimers(arr){const k=getK();k.timers=arr;setK(k)} const _activeTimers=new Map(); function addTimer(timer){ const arr=getTimers(); timer.id=Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6); timer.createdAt=new Date().toISOString(); if(!timer.source)timer.source='local'; arr.push(timer); setTimers(arr); scheduleTimer(timer); renderCronBar(); return timer; } function removeTimer(id){ if(_activeTimers.has(id)){clearTimeout(_activeTimers.get(id));_activeTimers.delete(id)} setTimers(getTimers().filter(t=>t.id!==id)); renderCronBar(); } function scheduleTimer(timer){ if(_activeTimers.has(timer.id))return; const remaining=new Date(timer.endsAt).getTime()-Date.now(); if(remaining<=0){fireTimer(timer);return} const tid=setTimeout(()=>fireTimer(timer),remaining); _activeTimers.set(timer.id,tid); } function fireTimer(timer){ _activeTimers.delete(timer.id); playAlarmSound();haptic('success'); sysBubble(`
[Timer] ${esc(timer.message||'Timer done')}
`,{ephemeral:true}); if(timer.source==='agent'){ const k=getK();const ag=getActiveAgent();const conn=ag?agentConnections.get(ag.id):null; if(k.mode==='agent'&&conn&&conn.connected)pipeline(`[Timer fired] ${timer.message||'Timer done'}`); } removeTimer(timer.id); } function checkTimers(){ const timers=getTimers(); timers.forEach(t=>{if(!_activeTimers.has(t.id))scheduleTimer(t)}); } function reconstitueTimers(){ const timers=getTimers(); timers.forEach(t=>scheduleTimer(t)); } // ── Pomodoro Timer ──────────────────────────────────── let _pomoInterval=null; function getPomodoro(){return getK().pomodoro||null} function setPomodoro(p){const k=getK();if(p)k.pomodoro=p;else delete k.pomodoro;setK(k)} function startPomodoro(config){ if(_pomoInterval){clearInterval(_pomoInterval);_pomoInterval=null} const id='pomo-'+Date.now(); const pomo={ id, tasks:(config.tasks||[]).map(t=>typeof t==='string'?{text:t,done:false}:t), phase:'focus', timeLeft:(config.duration||25)*60, duration:config.duration||25, breakDuration:config.breakDuration||5, round:1, totalRounds:config.totalRounds||4, paused:false, source:config.source||'local' }; setPomodoro(pomo); renderPomodoroBanner(); $('pomodoroBanner')?.classList.remove('hidden'); _pomoInterval=setInterval(tickPomodoro,1000); renderCronBar(); showToast(`Pomodoro started: ${pomo.duration}m focus`,'ok'); haptic('success'); sysBubble(`

`,{ephemeral:true}); notifyPomoAgent('focus_start',`Round ${pomo.round}/${pomo.totalRounds} started — ${pomo.duration}m focus`); } function tickPomodoro(){ const pomo=getPomodoro(); if(!pomo||pomo.paused)return; pomo.timeLeft--; if(pomo.timeLeft<=0){ if(pomo.phase==='focus'){ if(pomo.round>=pomo.totalRounds){ setPomodoro(pomo); endPomodoro('All rounds completed!'); return; } pomo.phase='break'; pomo.timeLeft=pomo.breakDuration*60; setPomodoro(pomo); renderPomodoroBanner(); renderCronBar(); playAlarmSound();haptic('success'); showToast('Break time!','ok'); sysBubble(`
Break ${pomo.breakDuration}m break — round ${pomo.round}/${pomo.totalRounds} complete
`,{ephemeral:true}); notifyPomoAgent('break_start',`Round ${pomo.round} done — ${pomo.breakDuration}m break`); } else { pomo.phase='focus'; pomo.round++; pomo.timeLeft=pomo.duration*60; setPomodoro(pomo); renderPomodoroBanner(); renderCronBar(); playAlarmSound();haptic('success'); showToast(`Round ${pomo.round} — Focus!`,'ok'); sysBubble(`
Focus Round ${pomo.round}/${pomo.totalRounds} — ${pomo.duration}m
`,{ephemeral:true}); notifyPomoAgent('focus_start',`Round ${pomo.round}/${pomo.totalRounds} started`); } } else { setPomodoro(pomo); renderPomodoroBanner(); // Update pill countdown in cronbar const pill=document.querySelector('.pomo-pill .pomo-cd'); if(pill)pill.textContent=fmtPomoCd(pomo.timeLeft); } } function pausePomodoro(){ const pomo=getPomodoro();if(!pomo)return; pomo.paused=true;setPomodoro(pomo); renderPomodoroBanner(); showToast('Pomodoro paused','ok'); } function resumePomodoro(){ const pomo=getPomodoro();if(!pomo)return; pomo.paused=false;setPomodoro(pomo); renderPomodoroBanner(); showToast('Pomodoro resumed','ok'); } function skipPomoPhase(){ const pomo=getPomodoro();if(!pomo)return; pomo.timeLeft=0;setPomodoro(pomo); tickPomodoro(); } function endPomodoro(reason){ const pomo=getPomodoro(); if(_pomoInterval){clearInterval(_pomoInterval);_pomoInterval=null} $('pomodoroBanner')?.classList.add('hidden'); if(pomo){ const doneCount=pomo.tasks.filter(t=>t.done).length; const summary=`Pomodoro complete! ${pomo.round} round${pomo.round>1?'s':''}, ${doneCount}/${pomo.tasks.length} tasks done.`; sysBubble(`
Pomodoro Done ${esc(reason||summary)}
`,{ephemeral:true}); notifyPomoAgent('completed',reason||summary); playAlarmSound();haptic('success'); } setPomodoro(null); renderCronBar(); if(reason)showToast(reason,'ok'); } function togglePomoTask(index){ const pomo=getPomodoro();if(!pomo||!pomo.tasks[index])return; pomo.tasks[index].done=!pomo.tasks[index].done; setPomodoro(pomo); renderPomodoroBanner(); notifyPomoAgent('task_checked',`Task "${pomo.tasks[index].text}" ${pomo.tasks[index].done?'completed':'unchecked'}`); } function renderPomodoroBanner(){ const pomo=getPomodoro();if(!pomo)return; const timerEl=$('pomoTimer'); const phaseEl=$('pomoPhase'); const tasksEl=$('pomoTasks'); const pauseBtn=$('pomoPauseBtn'); if(timerEl){ const m=Math.floor(pomo.timeLeft/60); const s=pomo.timeLeft%60; timerEl.textContent=String(m).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(s).padStart(2,'0'); } if(phaseEl){ phaseEl.textContent=pomo.phase==='focus'?'Focus':'Break'; phaseEl.className='pomo-phase '+(pomo.phase==='focus'?'focus':'break'); } if(pauseBtn)pauseBtn.textContent=pomo.paused?'Resume':'Pause'; if(tasksEl){ tasksEl.innerHTML=pomo.tasks.map((t,i)=> `

` ).join(''); } } function fmtPomoCd(sec){ if(sec>=3600)return Math.floor(sec/3600)+'h'+String(Math.floor((sec%3600)/60)).padStart(2,'0')+'m'; if(sec>=60)return Math.floor(sec/60)+'m'+String(sec%60).padStart(2,'0')+'s'; return sec+'s'; } function notifyPomoAgent(event,message){ const pomo=getPomodoro(); if(!pomo||pomo.source==='local')return; const k=getK(); if(k.mode==='agent'){ const ag=getActiveAgent(); const conn=ag?agentConnections.get(ag.id):null; if(conn&&conn.connected)pipeline(`[Pomodoro: ${event}] ${message}`); } } function restorePomodoro(){ const pomo=getPomodoro(); if(!pomo)return; $('pomodoroBanner')?.classList.remove('hidden'); renderPomodoroBanner(); if(!pomo.paused){ _pomoInterval=setInterval(tickPomodoro,1000); } } // ── Alarm Checker ─────────────────────────────────── function checkAlarms(){ const alarms=getAlarms(); if(!alarms.length)return; const now=new Date(); const hhmm=String(now.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0'); const today=now.toISOString().slice(0,10); alarms.forEach(alarm=>{ if(alarm.time!==hhmm||alarm.date!==today)return; const dedupKey='tc-alarm-'+alarm.id; if(localStorage.getItem(dedupKey)===today)return; localStorage.setItem(dedupKey,today); fireAlarm(alarm); }); } function fireAlarm(alarm){ playAlarmSound();haptic('success'); sysBubble(`

`,{ephemeral:true}); break; } // Notify agent if source is agent and gateway connected if(job.source==='agent'&&gwConnected&&action!=='pipeline'){ pipeline(`[Cron fired: ${label}] action=${action} — ${msg}`); } sysBubble(`
Cron fired: ${esc(label)} (${action})

}); // A11y: focus trap + Escape for cron modal document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ const modal=$('cronModal'); if(!modal||modal.classList.contains('hidden')||modal.dataset.open!=='1')return; if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();closeCronModal();return;} if(e.key!=='Tab')return; // ── /chron Wizard ─────────────────────────────────── let _chronWizardActive=false,_chronTimeout=null,_chronHistory=[]; const CHRON_WIZARD_PROMPT=`You are TealClaw's cron job setup wizard. Help the user create a scheduled message that will be sent to their AI agent at specific times. Gather the following one question at a time: 1. What message should be sent? (e.g. "Give me a morning briefing") 2. What time(s)? (e.g. "9am", "9:00 and 5:30pm") 3. Recurring or one-time? 4. If recurring: which days? (e.g. "weekdays", "every day", "mon wed fri") 5. } } } // ── Mic/Send Toggle ─────────────────────────────── function updateActionBtn(){ const hasText=$('textIn').value.trim().length>0; const hasFiles=pendingFiles.length>0; const streaming=!!_sending; const willQueue=shouldStageInsteadOfSend()&&hasText&&!hasFiles; // if files attached, we force send (avoids losing attachments) $('micBtn').classList.toggle('hidden',hasText||hasFiles||streaming); $('sendBtn').classList.toggle('hidden',(!hasText&&!hasFiles)||streaming); $('stopStreamBtn').classList.toggle('hidden',!streaming); $('sendBtn').classList.toggle('queueing',willQueue); } function stopStreaming(){ if(window._tcActiveReader){try{window._tcActiveReader.cancel()}catch{}} if(window._tcAbort){try{window._tcAbort.abort()}catch{}} _sending=false; updateActionBtn(); flushQueuedAutoSend(); } $('stopStreamBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>stopStreaming()); // ── Send + Staged Queue ───────────────────────────── let _sending=false; const _queuedAutoSends=[]; // [{id,text,ts,open}] let _queueFlushTimer=null; let _queueWatchTimer=null; let _queueSendAllOnce=false; // if true, next flush will send ALL staged messages in a single pass function isTTSPlaying(){ try{return !!audio && !audio.paused && !audio.ended && audio.currentTime>0}catch{return false} } function shouldStageInsteadOfSend(){ // Stage when a request is in-flight; optionally also stage while TTS is speaking. if(_sending)return true; const k=getK(); if(k.stageWhileTTSPlaying===false)return false; return isTTSPlaying(); } function renderSendQueue(){ const qEl=$('sendQueue'); const sb=$('sendBtn'); const badge=$('queueBadge'); const count=_queuedAutoSends.length; if(badge){badge.textContent=String(count)} if(sb){sb.classList.toggle('has-queue',count>0)} if(count===0){qEl.classList.add('hidden');qEl.innerHTML='';return} qEl.classList.remove('hidden'); qEl.innerHTML=`
Queued (${count})
${count>1?``:''} ${(_queueSendAllOnce&&count>1)?``:''}
${_queuedAutoSends.map((m,idx)=>`
${esc(m.text||'')}
Staged${_queueSendAllOnce&&count>1?' • send-all armed':''}
`).join('')}
`; qEl.querySelector('#queueFireNowBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{ // Force queue flush even if TealClaw got stuck in a "busy" state. try{stopTTSPlayback()}catch{} try{stopStreaming()}catch{} setTimeout(()=>{try{flushQueuedAutoSend()}catch{}},60); }); qEl.querySelector('#queueSendAllBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{_queueSendAllOnce=true;renderSendQueue();}); qEl.querySelector('#queueDisarmBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{_queueSendAllOnce=false;renderSendQueue();}); const idx=+btn.dataset.idx; if(Number.isFinite(idx))_queuedAutoSends.splice(idx,1); if(!_queuedAutoSends.length)clearQueueWatchTimer(); renderSendQueue();updateActionBtn(); })); qEl.querySelectorAll('.send-queue-item').forEach(row=>row.addEventListener('click',()=>{ const idx=+row.dataset.idx; if(!Number.isFinite(idx))return; _queuedAutoSends[idx].open=!_queuedAutoSends[idx].open; renderSendQueue(); })); } function queueAutoSendText(text){ const t=(text||'').trim(); if(!t)return; const last=_queuedAutoSends[_queuedAutoSends.length-1]; if(last&&last.text===t)return; _queuedAutoSends.push({id:crypto.randomUUID?.()||String(Date.now())+Math.random(),text:t,ts:Date.now(),open:false}); ensureQueueWatchTimer(); renderSendQueue(); updateActionBtn(); } function clearQueueFlushTimer(){ if(_queueFlushTimer){try{clearTimeout(_queueFlushTimer)}catch{};_queueFlushTimer=null} } function clearQueueWatchTimer(){ if(_queueWatchTimer){try{clearInterval(_queueWatchTimer)}catch{};_queueWatchTimer=null} } function ensureQueueWatchTimer(){ if(_queueWatchTimer)return; _queueWatchTimer=setInterval(()=>{ if(!_queuedAutoSends.length){clearQueueWatchTimer();return} if(_sending||recording||recorder?.state==='recording')return; // If nothing is playing and we're idle, the queue should eventually flush. if(!isTTSPlaying()){ try{flushQueuedAutoSend()}catch{} } },500); } function maybeFlushQueueAfterTTS(){ clearQueueFlushTimer(); if(!_queuedAutoSends.length)return; if(_sending||recording||recorder?.state==='recording')return; // If TTS is currently playing, flush when it's ~75% complete for better "in sync" flow. if(isTTSPlaying()){ const dur=audio.duration; const cur=audio.currentTime; if(Number.isFinite(dur)&&dur>0.25){ const target=Math.min(dur,Math.max(cur,0)+Math.max(0.05,dur*0.75-cur)); const ms=Math.max(50,Math.floor((target-cur)*1000)); _queueFlushTimer=setTimeout(()=>{flushQueuedAutoSend();},ms); return; } } flushQueuedAutoSend(); } function flushQueuedAutoSend(){ clearQueueFlushTimer(); if(_sending||recording||recorder?.state==='recording')return; if(!_queuedAutoSends.length){clearQueueWatchTimer();return} // "Send all at once" option: collapse the staged queue into one outbound message. // This avoids concurrent sends and preserves provider-at-send-time behavior. if(_queueSendAllOnce && _queuedAutoSends.length>1){ const all=_queuedAutoSends.splice(0,_queuedAutoSends.length).map(m=>(m?.text||'').trim()).filter(Boolean); send(); return; } const next=_queuedAutoSends.shift(); // Reset one-shot send-all flag at the start of a new queue pass if(_queuedAutoSends.length===0)_queueSendAllOnce=false; renderSendQueue(); updateActionBtn(); if(!next||!next.text)return; $('textIn').value=next.text; $('textIn').dispatchEvent(new Event('input')); send(); } async function send(){ if(_sending){ const draft=$('textIn').value.trim(); // Don't drop quick sends while prior response is still finalizing (e.g., right after TTS Stop) if(draft)queueAutoSendText(draft); }); // ── Multi-File Stack ───────────────────────────── let pendingFiles=[]; function fmtSize(bytes){ if(bytes<1024)return bytes+'B'; if(bytes<1048576)return(bytes/1024).toFixed(1)+'KB'; return(bytes/1048576).toFixed(1)+'MB';     ${esc(f.name)}×
    `; } if(f.type==='document'){ return `
    ${fileIcon(f.name)}${esc(f.name)}${f.size?`${fmtSize(f.size)}`:''}${f.processing?'':''}×
    `; } const sizeLabel=f.origSize&&f.compSize&&f.compSize${f.dataUrl?``:'
    '+esc(f.name)+'
    '}${sizeLabel?`${sizeLabel}`:''}${f.processing?'':''}×`; }).join(''); el.querySelectorAll('.file-stack-rm').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{ pendingFiles.splice(+b.dataset.idx,1);renderFileStack();updateActionBtn(); })); } function clearAllFiles(){pendingFiles=[];renderFileStack()} // ── Image Compression ────────────────────────────── function compressImage(dataUrl,file){ const TARGET_BYTES=100*1024; // ~100kb target return new Promise(resolve=>{ const img=new Image(); img.onload=()=>{ let w=img.width,h=img.height; // Scale down to fit within ~100kb: start with 1568px max, shrink further if needed let maxDim=1568; const canvas=document.createElement('canvas'); const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d'); function tryCompress(dim,q){ let tw=w,th=h; if(tw>dim||th>dim){const s=Math.min(dim/tw,dim/th);tw=Math.round(tw*s);th=Math.round(th*s)} canvas.width=tw;canvas.height=th; ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,tw,th); return canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',q); } // Try progressively lower quality/size until under target let result=tryCompress(maxDim,0.75); if(result.length*0.75>TARGET_BYTES){result=tryCompress(1024,0.65)} if(result.length*0.75>TARGET_BYTES){result=tryCompress(800,0.55)} if(result.length*0.75>TARGET_BYTES){result=tryCompress(640,0.45)} resolve({dataUrl:result,compSize:Math.round(result.length*0.75)}); }; img.onerror=()=>resolve({dataUrl,compSize:file.size}); img.src=dataUrl; }); } // ── HEIC/HEIF Conversion (lazy-loaded) ────────────── let _heicLoaded=false; function loadHeic2Any(){ if(_heicLoaded)return Promise.resolve(); return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const s=document.createElement('script'); s.src='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/heic2any/0.0.4/heic2any.min.js'; s.onload=()=>{_heicLoaded=true;resolve()}; s.onerror=reject; document.head.appendChild(s); }); } async function convertHeicToBlob(file){ try{ await loadHeic2Any(); const blob=await window.heic2any({blob:file,toType:'image/jpeg',quality:0.8}); return Array.isArray(blob)?blob[0]:blob; }catch(e){ console.warn('HEIC conversion failed:',e); return null; } } function isHeic(file,ext){ return file.type==='image/heic'||file.type==='image/heif'||ext==='heic'||ext==='heif'; } // ── PDF to Markdown (lazy-load pdf.js) ────────────── let pdfjsLoaded=false; function loadPdfJs(){ if(pdfjsLoaded)return Promise.resolve(); return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const s=document.createElement('script'); s2.src='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.min.js'; s2.onload=()=>{pdfjsLoaded=true;window.pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';resolve()}; s2.onerror=reject; document.head.appendChild(s2); }; s.onload=()=>{pdfjsLoaded=true;resolve()}; document.head.appendChild(s); }); } async function pdfToMarkdown(arrayBuffer,fileName){ await loadPdfJs(); const lib=window.pdfjsLib; if(!lib)throw new Error('pdf.js failed to load'); lib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc=lib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc||'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js'; const text=content.items.map(it=>it.str).join(' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(); if(text)pages.push(`## Page ${i}\n\n${text}`); } const header=`# ${fileName}\n\n*${pdf.numPages} pages${pdf.numPages>maxPages?` (showing first ${maxPages})`:''} — converted to text*\n\n`; return header+pages.join('\n\n---\n\n'); } // ── Turndown (HTML→Markdown, lazy-load) ────────────── let turndownLoaded=false; function loadTurndown(){ if(turndownLoaded)return Promise.resolve(); return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const s=document.createElement('script'); s.src='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/turndown/7.2.0/turndown.js'; s.onload=()=>{turndownLoaded=true;resolve()}; s.onerror=reject; document.head.appendChild(s); }); } function htmlToMarkdown(html){ if(!window.TurndownService)return html.replace(/<[^>]+>/g,''); } // ── DOCX to Markdown (lazy-load mammoth.js + turndown) ─ let mammothLoaded=false; function loadMammoth(){ if(mammothLoaded)return Promise.resolve(); return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const s=document.createElement('script'); } // ── HTML file to Markdown (lazy-load turndown) ──────── async function htmlFileToMarkdown(text,fileName){ await loadTurndown(); const md=htmlToMarkdown(text); return `# ${fileName}\n\n*Converted from HTML*\n\n${md}`; } // ── XLSX/XLS to Markdown (lazy-load SheetJS) ────────── let sheetjsLoaded=false; function loadSheetJS(){ if(sheetjsLoaded)return Promise.resolve(); return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const s=document.createElement('script'); const rows=window.XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws,{header:1,defval:''}); if(!rows.length)continue; let md=`## ${sheetName}\n\n`; const header=rows[0].map(c=>String(c??'')); md+='| '+header.join(' | ')+' |\n'; const zip=await window.JSZip.loadAsync(arrayBuffer); const slides=[]; // PPTX slides are in ppt/slides/slide1.xml, slide2.xml, etc. const slideFiles=Object.keys(zip.files).filter(f=>f.match(/^ppt\/slides\/slide\d+\.xml$/)).sort((a,b)=>{ const na=parseInt(a.match(/slide(\d+)/)[1]),nb=parseInt(b.match(/slide(\d+)/)[1]); for(const path of noteFiles){ const xml=await zip.file(path).async('text'); const texts=[]; const re=/([^<]*)<\/a:t>/g; let m;while((m=re.exec(xml))!==null){if(m[1].trim()&&m[1]!=='*')texts.push(m[1])} if(texts.length){ const num=path.match(/notesSlide(\d+)/)[1]; const slideIdx=slides.findIndex(s=>s.startsWith(`## Slide ${num}`)); if(slideIdx>=0)slides[slideIdx]+=`\n\n> **Speaker Notes:** ${texts.join(' ')}`; } } return `# ${fileName}\n\n*${slideFiles.length} slides — converted from PPTX*\n\n${slides.join('\n\n---\n\n')}`; } // ── EXIF Metadata (lazy-load exif-js) ───────────────── let exifLoaded=false; function loadExif(){ if(exifLoaded)return Promise.resolve(); return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const s=document.createElement('script'); if(!window.EXIF)return null; return new Promise(resolve=>{ const reader=new FileReader(); reader.onload=e=>{ try{ const exif=window.EXIF.readFromBinaryFile(e.target.result); if(!exif||!Object.keys(exif).length){resolve(null);return} const pick={}; const fields={Make:'Camera Make',Model:'Camera Model',DateTime:'Date Taken',ExposureTime:'Exposure',FNumber:'F-Stop',ISOSpeedRatings:'ISO',FocalLength:'Focal Length',GPSLatitude:'GPS Lat',GPSLongitude:'GPS Lon',GPSAltitude:'GPS Alt',ImageWidth:'Width',ImageHeight:'Height',Software:'Software',Artist:'Artist',Copyright:'Copyright',LensModel:'Lens'}; for(const[k,label] of Object.entries(fields)){ if(exif[k]!==undefined&&exif[k]!==null){ let v=exif[k]; if(k==='ExposureTime'&&typeof v==='number')v=v<1?`1/${Math.round(1/v)}s`:`${v}s`; else if(k==='FNumber'&&typeof v==='number')v=`f/${v}`; else if(k==='FocalLength'&&typeof v==='number')v=`${v}mm`; else if((k==='GPSLatitude'||k==='GPSLongitude')&&Array.isArray(v))v=`${v[0]}° ${v[1]}' ${v[2]}"`; else if(k==='GPSAltitude'&&typeof v==='number')v=`${Math.round(v)}m`; else if(k==='ISOSpeedRatings'&&Array.isArray(v))v=v[0]; pick[label]=String(v); } } resolve(Object.keys(pick).length?pick:null); }catch{resolve(null)} }; reader.onerror=()=>resolve(null); reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file); }); }catch{return null} } function exifToMarkdown(exif,fileName){ if(!exif)return ''; let md=`\n\n**EXIF Metadata (${fileName}):**\n`; for(const[k,v] of Object.entries(exif))md+=`- ${k}: ${v}\n`; return md; } // ── CSV/TSV to Markdown Table ─────────────────────── function csvToMarkdown(text,fileName){ const lines=text.trim().split('\n'); if(lines.length<2)return text; // Detect delimiter const delim=lines[0].includes('\t')?'\t':','; const rows=lines.map(line=>{ const cells=[];let cell='',inQuote=false; for(let i=0;i'---'); const table=[header,sep,...rows.slice(1)].map(r=>'| '+r.join(' | ')+' |').join('\n'); return `# ${fileName}\n\n*${rows.length-1} rows, ${header.length} columns*\n\n${table}`; } // ── File Attach ──────────────────────────────────── $('attachBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>$('fileIn').click()); $('fileIn').addEventListener('change',e=>{ if(e.target.files){for(const f of e.target.files)handleFile(f)} $('fileIn').value=''; }); document.addEventListener('paste',e=>{ const items=e.clipboardData?.items;if(!items)return; for(const item of items){if(item.kind==='file'){e.preventDefault();handleFile(item.getAsFile())}} }); // ── Full-window drag and drop ─────────────────────── let dragCounter=0;     `); const last=document.querySelector('.chat-bubble:last-child'); const inp=last?.querySelector('#convImportPass'); const btn=last?.querySelector('#convImportBtn'); if(!inp||!btn)return; setTimeout(()=>inp.focus(),100); } // ── Handle File (with auto-conversion) ────────────── async function handleFile(file){ const name=file.name||'unnamed'; const ext=name.split('.').pop().toLowerCase(); // JSON config file import (from agent or manual) if(ext==='json'||file.type==='application/json'){ const reader=new FileReader();     ${report.failed.length} field(s) failed to apply. Use Retry below.

    `:''; sysBubble(`

    Loaded ${report.count} settings from ${esc(name)}:

    ${parts.join(', ')||'Configuration updated'}

    ${statusLine}

    ${cfg.mode==='agent'?'Mode set to Agent -- routing through your OpenClaw gateway.':(cfg.mode==='telegram-relay'?'Mode set to Telegram Relay -- messages route through Telegram transport.':'You\'re ready to chat!')}

    `); showToast(report.failed.length?`Imported ${report.count} settings (${report.failed.length} failed)`:`Imported ${report.count} settings`,report.failed.length?'err':'ok'); if(report.failed.length){ showImportFailureCard(report.failed,buildRetryConfigForFailedKeys(report.failed),'file import'); } if(cfg.botGreeting){ setTimeout(()=>sysBubble(`
    ${renderMd(cfg.botGreeting)}
    `),500); } return; } }catch{} // Not a config file — paste as text document const txt=e.target.result?.slice(0,32000); appendDocText(name,txt); }; reader.readAsText(file); return; } // Images — auto-convert and optimize on import if(file.type?.startsWith('image/')){ // Check for TC QR code before normal image processing if(typeof handleQrImage==='function'){ const qrUrl=await new Promise(r=>{const rd=new FileReader();rd.onload=ev=>r(ev.target.result);rd.onerror=()=>r(null);rd.readAsDataURL(file)}); // Extract EXIF in parallel const exifPromise=(ext==='jpg'||ext==='jpeg'||ext==='tiff'||ext==='tif')?extractExif(file):Promise.resolve(null); // Convert HEIC/HEIF to JPEG blob first let imgFile=file; return; } // PDF — convert to markdown text if(ext==='pdf'||file.type==='application/pdf'){ const idx=pendingFiles.length; pendingFiles.push({name,type:'document',processing:true,size:file.size}); renderFileStack();updateActionBtn(); }catch(err){ // Fallback: just note the file pendingFiles[idx]={name,type:'document',processing:false,size:file.size,text:`[Could not extract text from ${name}: ${err.message}]`}; renderFileStack(); showToast(`PDF conversion failed — file attached as-is`,'err'); } return; } // DOCX — convert to markdown text if(ext==='docx'||file.type==='application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document'){ const idx=pendingFiles.length; pendingFiles.push({name,type:'document',processing:true,size:file.size}); pendingFiles.push({name,type:'document',processing:true,size:file.size}); renderFileStack();updateActionBtn(); try{ const buf=await file.arrayBuffer(); const md=await xlsxToMarkdown(buf,name); pendingFiles[idx]={name,type:'document',processing:false,size:file.size,text:md}; } }; reader2.readAsText(file); return; } // All other text-based files — read as text document const textExts=['txt','md','js','ts','jsx','tsx','py','rb','go','rs','c','cpp','h','hpp','java','kt','swift','m','css','xml','yaml','yml','toml','ini','cfg','env','sh','bash','zsh','sql','r','log','rtf','tex','makefile','dockerfile','gitignore']; return; } // Unknown binary file — add as document placeholder pendingFiles.push({name,type:'document',processing:false,size:file.size,text:`[Binary file: ${name} (${fmtSize(file.size)})]`}); renderFileStack();updateActionBtn(); } function appendDocText(name,txt){ pendingFiles.push({name,type:'document',processing:false,size:txt?.length||0,text:txt}); renderFileStack();updateActionBtn(); } // ── Microphone ───────────────────────────────────── const micBtn=$('micBtn'),hint=$('inputHint'); function createAudioRecorder(audioStream,onData,onStop){ const candidates=['audio/webm;codecs=opus','audio/webm','audio/mp4','audio/ogg;codecs=opus']; let mimeType=''; if(window.MediaRecorder&&MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported){ mimeType=candidates.find(t=>MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(t))||''; } const rec=mimeType?new MediaRecorder(audioStream,{mimeType}):new MediaRecorder(audioStream); rec.ondataavailable=onData; rec.onstop=onStop; return rec; } let _recStartMs=0; function isMeaningfulTranscript(rawText,durationMs){ const k=getK?.()||{}; const minDur=Number.isFinite(+k.voiceMinDurationMs)?Math.max(0,+k.voiceMinDurationMs):700; const minChars=Number.isFinite(+k.voiceMinChars)?Math.max(0,+k.voiceMinChars):12; const minWords=Number.isFinite(+k.voiceMinWords)?Math.max(0,+k.voiceMinWords):3; const allowShort = (k.voiceAllowShortCommands!==undefined) ? !!k.voiceAllowShortCommands : true; const defaultWhitelist=['stop','cancel','restart','send','yes','no','do it','lets go','let\'s go','hurry up']; const whitelist = Array.isArray(k.voiceCommandWhitelist) ? k.voiceCommandWhitelist : defaultWhitelist; const t=(rawText||'').trim(); if(!t)return false; if(/^[\s\p{P}]+$/u.test(t))return false; // punctuation-only (".", "...", etc.) // Normalize to simple phrase for matching + counting const norm=t .toLowerCase() .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s']/gu,' ') // keep letters/numbers/spaces/apostrophe .replace(/\s+/g,' ') .trim(); if(!norm)return false; // If it's mostly non-latin letters and short, ignore (prevents weird CJK noise) unless explicitly whitelisted. const letters=[...norm].filter(ch=>/\p{L}/u.test(ch)); const latinLetters=letters.filter(ch=>/\p{Script=Latin}/u.test(ch)); const nonLatinRatio = letters.length ? (1 - (latinLetters.length/letters.length)) : 0; // Whitelist short commands and allow polite/urgent suffixes. // Includes a very small fuzzy-match so "stop" can still pass if Whisper returns "scott". if(allowShort && whitelist?.length){ const cleaned = norm.replace(/\b(please|pls|plz|now)\b/g,'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(); const alsoAllow = cleaned.replace(/\b(ok|okay)\b/g,'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(); const candidates=[cleaned,alsoAllow].filter(Boolean); const wl=whitelist.map(s=>String(s||'').toLowerCase().trim()).filter(Boolean); const editDist=(a,b)=>{ if(a===b)return 0; const al=a.length,bl=b.length; if(!al)return bl; if(!bl)return al; // small DP, strings are tiny here const dp=new Array(bl+1); for(let j=0;j<=bl;j++)dp[j]=j; for(let i=1;i<=al;i++){ let prev=dp[0]; dp[0]=i; const ai=a.charCodeAt(i-1); for(let j=1;j<=bl;j++){ const tmp=dp[j]; const cost=(ai===b.charCodeAt(j-1))?0:1; dp[j]=Math.min( dp[j]+1, // deletion dp[j-1]+1, // insertion prev+cost // substitution ); prev=tmp; } } return dp[bl]; }; const fuzzyOk=(c,w)=>{ // Only fuzzy-match short phrases (1–2 words) to avoid accidental sends. const cw=c.split(' ').filter(Boolean); const ww=w.split(' ').filter(Boolean); if(cw.length>2||ww.length>2)return false; if(c.length>14||w.length>14)return false; const d=editDist(c,w); const max=Math.max(c.length,w.length); const th=(max<=5)?1:(max<=9?2:2); return d<=th; }; for(const c of candidates){ if(wl.includes(c))return true; // Fuzzy match (very conservative) for(const w of wl){ if(fuzzyOk(c,w))return true; } } } if(nonLatinRatio>0.6 && norm.length<20)return false; if(durationMs!=null&&durationMs=minWords)return true; if(norm.length>=minChars)return true; return false; } async function initMic(){ try{ stream=await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({audio:{echoCancellation:true,noiseSuppression:true}}); recorder=createAudioRecorder(stream,e=>{if(e.data.size>0)chunks.push(e.data)},async()=>{ const dur=(_recStartMs?Date.now()-_recStartMs:null); }catch{showToast('Mic access denied','err');return false} } micBtn.addEventListener('mousedown',async e=>{e.preventDefault();if(recording||recorder?.state==='recording')return;if(!recorder&&!await initMic())return;chunks=[];_recStartMs=Date.now();recorder.start();recording=true;micBtn.classList.add('recording');clearLiveSubtitle();haptic('medium')}); micBtn.addEventListener('touchend',e=>{e.preventDefault();stopRec()}); function stopRec(){if(!recorder||!recording)return;recorder.stop();recording=false;micBtn.classList.remove('recording');haptic('light')} // ── Claw Mode (dedicated toggle button) ───────────── function toggleClawMode(){ const btn=$('clawToggle'); const TG_RELAY_INBOUND_EVENTS=[ 'chat', 'message.receive', 'message', 'message.inbound', 'channel.message', 'channel.message.inbound', 'channel.message.receive', 'channel.inbound', 'telegram.message', 'telegram.inbound', 'telegram.update', 'relay.telegram.inbound' ]; function tgRelayCursorKey(k,agentId){ const cfg=k||getK(); const chat=String(cfg.tgChatId||'unknown').trim(); const agent=String(agentId||'main').trim().toLowerCase()||'main'; return `tc-tg-relay-gw-cursor:${agent}:${chat}`; } function getTelegramRelayCursor(k,agentId){ const raw=localStorage.getItem(tgRelayCursorKey(k,agentId)); const n=Number(raw||0); return Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0?n:0; } function setTelegramRelayCursor(k,agentId,timestampMs){ const n=Math.max(0,Number(timestampMs)||0); localStorage.setItem(tgRelayCursorKey(k,agentId),String(n)); } function getTelegramTag(){ const k=getK(); const name=k.deviceName||getDeviceTag().name||''; return name?`[${name}] `:''; } function rememberRelayOutbound(chatId,text){ if(!text)return; const now=Date.now(); _tgRelayRecentOutbound=_tgRelayRecentOutbound.filter(x=>now-x.ts<180000); _tgRelayRecentOutbound.push({chatId:String(chatId||''),text:String(text),ts:now}); if(_tgRelayRecentOutbound.length>80)_tgRelayRecentOutbound=_tgRelayRecentOutbound.slice(-80); } function hasSeenRelayMessage(id){ return !!id&&_tgRelaySeen.includes(String(id)); } function extractTelegramMessageText(msg){ if(!msg)return ''; if(typeof msg==='string')return msg; if(msg.text)return msg.text; if(msg.sticker)return '[Telegram sticker]'; return ''; } function telegramSenderName(msg){ const from=msg?.from||{}; const parts=[from.first_name,from.last_name].filter(Boolean); if(parts.length)return parts.join(' '); if(from.username)return '@'+from.username; return from.id?`user ${from.id}`:'Telegram'; } function sanitizeRelayToken(v){ return String(v||'').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9:_-]+/g,''); } function getRelayGatewayAgent(){ const agents=getAgents(); if(typeof conn.supportsMethod==='function')return conn.supportsMethod(method); const methods=conn.serverInfo?.features?.methods; return Array.isArray(methods)&&methods.includes(method); } function isUnknownRelayMethodError(err){ const msg=String(err?.message||err||''); return /unknown method|method not found|INVALID_REQUEST|not supported/i.test(msg); } async function tryRelayMethods(conn,candidates=[]){ let lastErr=null; for(const c of candidates){ if(!c?.method)continue; if(isUnknownRelayMethodError(err))continue; throw err; } } if(lastErr)throw lastErr; return null; } function extractTextFromGatewayMessage(msg){ if(!msg)return ''; if(typeof msg==='string')return msg; const n=Number(v); if(Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0)return n>1e12?n:n*1000; const p=Date.parse(String(v)); return Number.isFinite(p)?p:Date.now(); } function extractRelayChatId(payload){ if(!payload)return ''; } function parseRelayInboundEvent(event,payload,conn){ const k=getK(); if(!payload)return null; const ev=String(event||'').toLowerCase(); if(ev==='chat'){ const agent=getRelayGatewayAgent(); if(!agent)return null; } function parseRelayHistoryMessage(msg,sessionKey){ const k=getK(); if(!msg||String(msg.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='user')return null; const text=extractTextFromGatewayMessage(msg).trim(); if(!text)return null; } async function relayFetchHistory(conn,sessionKey,limit=120){ const attempts=[ {method:'chat.history',params:{sessionKey,limit}}, {method:'history.get',params:{sessionKey,limit}}, {method:'chat.sync',params:{sessionKey,limit}}, {method:'message.history',params:{sessionKey,limit}} ]; return{messages,method:a.method}; }catch(err){ if(isUnknownRelayMethodError(err))continue; throw err; } } return{messages:[],method:null}; } async function reconcileTelegramRelayStartup(conn,agent){ const k=getK(); if(!isTelegramRelayEnabled(k)||k.tgRelayReceive===false||!hasTelegramConfig(k)||!conn?.connected)return; _tgRelayBridge.startupState='syncing'; _tgRelayBridge.lastError=''; updateStatus(); const historyCapable=['chat.history','history.get','chat.sync','message.history'].some(m=>relaySupportsMethod(conn,m)); _tgRelayBridge.lastSyncAt=Date.now(); updateStatus(); return; } } } backlog.sort((a,b)=>a.timestamp-b.timestamp); backlog.forEach(item=>emitTelegramRelayInbound(item,{path:item.path,fromReconcile:true})); updateStatus(); } function unbindTelegramRelayGatewayHandlers(){ const conn=_tgRelayBridge.conn; if(conn){ _tgRelayBridge.handlers.forEach(({event,handler})=>conn.off(event,handler)); if(_tgRelayBridge.stateHandler)conn.off('_state',_tgRelayBridge.stateHandler); } _tgRelayBridge.conn=null; _tgRelayBridge.handlers=[]; _tgRelayBridge.stateHandler=null; } function bindTelegramRelayGatewayHandlers(conn,agent){ if(!conn)return; if(_tgRelayBridge.conn===conn&&_tgRelayBridge.handlers.length)return; }); const stateHandler=(state)=>{ if(!isTelegramRelayEnabled(getK()))return; _tgRelayRunning=(state==='connected'); if(state==='connected'){ _tgRelayBridge.startupState='connected'; reconcileTelegramRelayStartup(conn,agent).catch(err=>{ _tgRelayBridge.lastError=String(err?.message||err||'reconcile failed'); _tgRelayBridge.startupState='degraded'; updateStatus(); }); } else if(state==='connecting'){ _tgRelayBridge.startupState='connecting'; } else { _tgRelayBridge.startupState='gateway-offline'; } updateStatus(); }; conn.on('_state',stateHandler); _tgRelayBridge.stateHandler=stateHandler; if(conn.connected){ _tgRelayRunning=true; _tgRelayBridge.startupState='connected'; reconcileTelegramRelayStartup(conn,agent).catch(err=>{ _tgRelayBridge.lastError=String(err?.message||err||'reconcile failed'); _tgRelayBridge.startupState='degraded'; updateStatus(); }); }else{ _tgRelayRunning=false; _tgRelayBridge.startupState='connecting'; } } function stopTelegramRelayRuntime(){ _tgRelayRunning=false; _tgRelayBridge.startupState='idle'; _tgRelayBridge.startupState='idle'; updateStatus(); return; } const agent=getRelayGatewayAgent(); if(!agent?.url||!agent?.token){ _tgRelayRunning=false; _tgRelayBridge.startupState='gateway-required'; _tgRelayBridge.startupState='gateway-required'; _tgRelayBridge.lastError='Gateway connection unavailable.'; updateStatus(); return; } _tgRelayBridge.lastError=''; bindTelegramRelayGatewayHandlers(conn,agent); _tgRelayRunning=conn.connected; updateStatus(); } function telegramRelayRuntimeLabel(){ if(!isTelegramRelayEnabled(getK()))return 'off'; if(_tgRelayRunning)return 'gateway-live'; return _tgRelayBridge.startupState||'idle'; } async function sendTelegram(text){ const k=getK(); if(!k.tgEnabled||!k.tgToken||!k.tgChatId)return; const tagged=getTelegramTag()+text; try{ await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${k.tgToken}/sendMessage`,{ method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({chat_id:k.tgChatId,text:tagged,parse_mode:'Markdown'}) }); }catch(e){console.warn('Telegram send failed:',e)} } async function sendTelegramRelayViaGateway(content,chatId){ if(!content)return null; const agent=getRelayGatewayAgent(); if(!agent?.url||!agent?.token)return null; const conn=ensureAgentConnection(agent); if(!conn?.connected)return null; const target=String(chatId||'').trim(); if(!target)return null; const idem=crypto.randomUUID?crypto.randomUUID():`${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,10)}`; const attempt=await tryRelayMethods(conn,[ {method:'send',path:'gateway.send',params:{channel:'telegram',to:target,message:content,idempotencyKey:idem,bestEffort:true}}, {method:'message.send',path:'gateway.message.send',params:{channel:'telegram',target,message:content,idempotencyKey:idem,bestEffort:true}}, {method:'channel.send',path:'gateway.channel.send',params:{channel:'telegram',target,message:content,idempotencyKey:idem,bestEffort:true}}, {method:'channels.send',path:'gateway.channels.send',params:{channel:'telegram',target,message:content,idempotencyKey:idem,bestEffort:true}}, {method:'telegram.send',path:'gateway.telegram.send',params:{chatId:target,text:content,idempotencyKey:idem}} ]).catch(err=>{ _tgRelayBridge.lastError=String(err?.message||err||'relay send failed'); if(!apiKey&&!k.imageGenUrl){showToast('No image gen key — paste a Google AI or OpenRouter key','err');return null} // Google AI (Gemini / Nano Banana) — native API if(apiKey&&apiKey.startsWith('AIza')){ const model=k.imageGenModel||'gemini-2.5-flash-image'; if(res.status!==429)break; // Retry with backoff for rate limits const wait=(attempt+1)*2000; showToast(`Rate limited — retrying in ${(wait/1000)}s...`,'err'); await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,wait)); } if(!res.ok){ const err=await res.json().catch(()=>({})); const msg=err.error?.message||`Gemini: ${res.status}`; if(res.status===429)throw new Error('Gemini rate limit — free tier allows ~10 requests/min. Wait a moment and try again.'); throw new Error(msg); } const d=await res.json(); const resParts=d.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts||[]; const img=resParts.find(p=>p.inline_data); const txtPart=resParts.find(p=>p.text); if(img)return {url:`data:${img.inline_data.mime_type};base64,${img.inline_data.data}`,text:txtPart?.text||null}; return null; } // OpenRouter — OpenAI-compatible image API if(apiKey&&apiKey.startsWith('sk-or-v1-')){ const genUrl=k.imageGenUrl||'https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/images/generations'; if(k.imageGenModel)body.model=k.imageGenModel; const res=await fetch(k.imageGenUrl,{ method:'POST', headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${apiKey}`,'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify(body) }); function importValuesMatch(a,b){ try{return JSON.stringify(a)===JSON.stringify(b)} catch{return a===b} } function buildRetryConfigForFailedKeys(failed){ const retry={}; }); } function importConfig(cfg,opts={}){ const kBefore=getK(); const kNext={...kBefore}; const attempted=[]; for(const[from,to]of Object.entries(CONFIG_KEYS)){ if(cfg[from]!==undefined){ kNext[to]=cfg[from]; return; } if(!importValuesMatch(persisted[item.to],item.value)){ failed.push({...item,reason:'Value did not persist after save'}); return; } count++; }); // Mobile reliability fallback: retry failed keys one-by-one. if(storageOk&&failed.length){ const working={...getK()}; const stillFailed=[]; failed.forEach(item=>{ working[item.to]=item.value; const ok=setK(working); const check=getK(); if(ok&&importValuesMatch(check[item.to],item.value))count++; else stillFailed.push(item); return `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${a})`; } function hexToRgb(hex){ return `${parseInt(hex.slice(1,3),16)},${parseInt(hex.slice(3,5),16)},${parseInt(hex.slice(5,7),16)}`; } function darkenHex(hex,amt=0.15){ let r=parseInt(hex.slice(1,3),16),g=parseInt(hex.slice(3,5),16),b=parseInt(hex.slice(5,7),16); r=Math.max(0,Math.round(r*(1-amt)));g=Math.max(0,Math.round(g*(1-amt)));b=Math.max(0,Math.round(b*(1-amt))); return '#'+[r,g,b].map(c=>c.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join(''); } // Load Google Font for font picker choices const loadedFonts=new Set(); function loadChosenFont(id){ const googleFonts={ 'inter':'Inter:wght@400;500;600;700', 'nunito':'Nunito:wght@400;500;600;700', 'space-grotesk':'Space+Grotesk:wght@400;500;600;700', 'jetbrains':'JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500;600;700' }; // Remove url() with non-https/data schemes (blocks javascript:, data exfil via background) s=s.replace(/url\s*\(\s*(['"]?)(?!https?:|data:image\/)(\w+:)/gi,'url($1blocked:'); // Block data: URIs that aren't images (prevents data:text/html etc.) s=s.replace(/url\s*\(\s*(['"]?)data:(?!image\/)/gi,'url($1blocked:data:'); // Block mask-image and -webkit-mask-image with url() (exfil channel) s=s.replace(/(mask-image\s*:\s*)url\s*\(/gi,'$1/* blocked */ none'); // Block expression() and -moz-binding (legacy IE/FF XSS vectors) s=s.replace(/expression\s*\(/gi,'/* blocked */'); } else if(customStyle){ customStyle.remove(); } // GIF overlay sizing applyGifScaleStyles(); } // ── AES-GCM Encryption (Web Crypto API) ──────────── async function deriveKey(passphrase,salt){ const enc=new TextEncoder(); const keyMaterial=await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw',enc.encode(passphrase),'PBKDF2',false,['deriveKey']); return crypto.subtle.deriveKey({name:'PBKDF2',salt,iterations:100000,hash:'SHA-256'},keyMaterial,{name:'AES-GCM',length:256},false,['encrypt','decrypt']); } async function encryptConfig(json,passphrase){ const salt=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)); const iv=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12)); const key=await deriveKey(passphrase,salt); const ct=await crypto.subtle.encrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv},key,new TextEncoder().encode(json)); // Combine salt(16) + iv(12) + ciphertext, encode as base64 const buf=new Uint8Array(salt.length+iv.length+ct.byteLength); buf.set(salt);buf.set(iv,salt.length);buf.set(new Uint8Array(ct),salt.length+iv.length); return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...buf)).replace(/\+/g,'-').replace(/\//g,'_').replace(/=+$/,''); } async function decryptConfig(blob,passphrase){ let b64=blob.replace(/-/g,'+').replace(/_/g,'/'); while(b64.length%4)b64+='='; const raw=Uint8Array.from(atob(b64),c=>c.charCodeAt(0)); const salt=raw.slice(0,16); const iv=raw.slice(16,28); const ct=raw.slice(28); const key=await deriveKey(passphrase,salt); const pt=await crypto.subtle.decrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv},key,ct); return new TextDecoder().decode(pt); } function generatePassphrase(){ const chars='ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghjkmnpqrstuvwxyz23456789'; return Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(8)),b=>chars[b%chars.length]).join(''); } // URL hash: #gw=URL&token=TOKEN — auto-connect to a gateway function importGatewayFromHash(){ const h=location.hash; if(!h.includes('gw='))return false; const params=new URLSearchParams(h.slice(1)); let gwUrl=params.get('gw'); let token=params.get('token'); if(!gwUrl||!token)return false; gwUrl=gwUrl.trim(); token=token.trim(); if(!gwUrl||!token)return false; if(gwUrl.length>2048||token.length>4096){ showToast('Gateway link is too large','err'); return false; } // Scrub sensitive fragments (token) from the URL while preserving any query params. history.replaceState(null,'',location.pathname+location.search); // Validate gateway URL scheme to reduce token leakage risk. // Allow http only for localhost dev. let parsed=null; try{parsed=new URL(gwUrl)}catch(e){showToast('Invalid gateway URL','err');return false} const host=parsed.hostname||gwUrl; const isLocalhost=(host==='localhost'||host==='127.0.0.1'||host==='::1'); if(parsed.protocol!=='https:' && !(parsed.protocol==='http:' && isLocalhost)){ showToast('Gateway URL must be https (or http://localhost for dev)','err'); return false; } // Confirm before importing — this overwrites agent config const msg=`A gateway link wants to connect you to:\n\n${host}\n\nThis will set your active agent. Continue?`; if(!confirm(msg))return false; // Save agent and connect const agents=getAgents(); const agent={id:'link-gw',name:'My Agent',url:gwUrl,token:token,active:true}; const idx=agents.findIndex(a=>a.id==='link-gw'); $('chatScroll').innerHTML=''; onboardSuccess(gwUrl,'My Agent'); return true; } // URL hash fragment (#config=BASE64) never leaves the browser. // Supports both plain base64 JSON and encrypted (enc: prefix) configs. let pendingEncBlob=null; function importFromHash(){ if(importGatewayFromHash())return true; const h=location.hash; if(!h.startsWith('#config='))return false; const payload=h.slice(8); // Defensive cap: avoid giant hash payloads that can freeze the UI. if(payload.length>120000){ history.replaceState(null,'',location.pathname+location.search); showConfigImportConfirm(cfg); return true; }catch(e){console.warn('Config import failed:',e);return false} } function showDecryptPrompt(){ const overlay=$('decryptOverlay'); const input=$('decryptInput'); overlay.classList.remove('hidden'); input.value='';input.classList.remove('wrong'); setTimeout(()=>input.focus(),100); } function showConfigImportConfirm(cfg){ const fields=Object.keys(cfg).filter(k=>k!=='agents'&&k!=='_session');     Includes: ${esc(warns.join(', '))}

    `:''; sysBubble(`

    ${renderMd(cfg.botGreeting)}
    `),500); } }); } async function attemptDecrypt(){ const input=$('decryptInput'); const passphrase=input.value.trim(); if(passphrase.length<1)return; try{ const json=await decryptConfig(pendingEncBlob,passphrase); let pendingQrNonce=null; let pendingQrTs=null; let _qrOnboardImage=null; function parseQrPayload(data){ // tc-qr:1:TIMESTAMP:NONCE:ENCRYPTED_BLOB if(!data||!data.startsWith('tc-qr:1:'))return null; const parts=data.split(':'); return{ts,nonce,blob}; } function validateQrTimestamp(ts){ return(Math.floor(Date.now()/1000)-ts)<=300; } function showQrOnboardPrompt(nonce){ const overlay=$('qrOnboardOverlay'); const input=$('qrPassInput'); const inner=JSON.parse(json); // Verify inner timestamp and nonce match outer if(inner._ts!==pendingQrTs||inner._nonce!==pendingQrNonce){ $('qrHint').textContent='Invalid — QR code may have been tampered with'; $('qrHint').style.color='var(--err)'; input.classList.add('wrong'); haptic('error'); setTimeout(()=>{input.classList.remove('wrong');$('qrHint').style.color=''},400); input.value='';input.focus(); return; } // Strip verification fields before import delete inner._tcqr;delete inner._ts;delete inner._nonce; pendingQrBlob=null;pendingQrNonce=null;pendingQrTs=null; $('qrOnboardOverlay').classList.add('hidden'); haptic('success'); showConfigImportConfirm(inner); }catch{ input.classList.add('wrong'); haptic('error'); setTimeout(()=>input.classList.remove('wrong'),400); input.value='';input.focus(); } } $('qrUnlockBtn').addEventListener('click',attemptQrDecrypt); $('qrPassInput').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')attemptQrDecrypt()}); async function tryDecodeQrFromImage(dataUrl){ try{ await loadScript('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/jsqr@1.4.0/dist/jsQR.js'); const img=new Image(); await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{img.onload=resolve;img.onerror=reject;img.src=dataUrl}); const canvas=document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width=img.naturalWidth;canvas.height=img.naturalHeight; function buildConfigPayload(includeSensitive=false){ const k=getK(); const agents=getAgents(); const out={}; for(const[key,val]of Object.entries(k)){ if(val===''||val===null||val===undefined)continue; if(CONFIG_DEFAULTS[key]!==undefined&&JSON.stringify(CONFIG_DEFAULTS[key])===JSON.stringify(val))continue; out[key]=val; } if(agents.length)out.agents=agents; if(!includeSensitive){ const sensitive=['aiKey','whisperKey','tgToken','imageGenKey','pinHash','pinCode']; sensitive.forEach(s=>{delete out[s]}); if(out.agents)out.agents=out.agents.map(a=>({...a,token:undefined})); } return JSON.stringify(out); } function exportConfigUrl(includeSensitive=false){ return location.origin+'/#config='+btoa(buildConfigPayload(includeSensitive)); } async function exportEncryptedUrl(includeSensitive=true){ const passphrase=generatePassphrase(); const json=buildConfigPayload(includeSensitive); const blob=await encryptConfig(json,passphrase); return{url:location.origin+'/#config=enc:'+blob,passphrase}; } // ── Password Lock (hashed storage) ──────────────── let chatLocked=false; let lastActivity=Date.now(); const idleTimeoutMs=15*60*1000; // 15 minutes async function hashPin(pin){ const data=new TextEncoder().encode(pin+'tealclaw-pin-salt'); const hash=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',data); return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hash)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join(''); } // Migrate plaintext pinCode → pinHash on load (async function migratePinCode(){ const k=getK(); $('chatScroll').classList.add('locked'); $('textIn').placeholder='Enter password...'; addSystemBubble('This chat is password-protected. Type your password to unlock.'); } } async function handleChatLockInput(text){ // Called from send() when chatLocked is true const k=getK(); const h=await hashPin(text); if(h===k.pinHash){ chatLocked=false; $('chatScroll').classList.remove('locked'); $('textIn').placeholder=''; addSystemBubble('Unlocked!'); haptic('success'); lastActivity=Date.now(); } else { addSystemBubble('Wrong password. Try again.'); haptic('error'); } } function touchActivity(){lastActivity=Date.now()} // ── BLE Beacon Proximity Gate ───────────────────── let bleConnected=false; let _bleDevice=null; let _bleReconnectTimer=null; function bleSupported(){return!!navigator.bluetooth} function bleGateActive(){const k=getK();return!!k.bleEnabled&&!!k.bleDeviceId} function bleGateBlocking(){return bleGateActive()&&!bleConnected} async function blePairDevice(){ if(!bleSupported()){showToast('Bluetooth not available in this browser','err');return} try{ const device=await navigator.bluetooth.requestDevice({acceptAllDevices:true,optionalServices:['heart_rate']}); const server=await device.gatt.connect(); _bleDevice=device; bleConnected=true; device.addEventListener('gattserverdisconnected',_onBleDisconnect); const k=getK(); k.bleEnabled=true; k.bleDeviceId=device.id; k.bleDeviceName=device.name||'BLE Device'; setK(k); document.body.classList.remove('ble-locked'); $('textIn').disabled=false;$('textIn').placeholder=''; showToast(`Paired: ${k.bleDeviceName}`,'ok'); updateStatus(); if(typeof renderSheetContent==='function'){try{renderSheetContent()}catch(e){}} }catch(e){ if(e.name!=='NotFoundError')showToast('BLE pairing failed: '+e.message,'err'); } } function bleUnpairDevice(){ if(_bleReconnectTimer){clearInterval(_bleReconnectTimer);_bleReconnectTimer=null} if(_bleDevice&&_bleDevice.gatt.connected)try{_bleDevice.gatt.disconnect()}catch(e){} _bleDevice=null; bleConnected=false; const k=getK(); delete k.bleEnabled;delete k.bleDeviceId;delete k.bleDeviceName; setK(k); document.body.classList.remove('ble-locked'); $('textIn').disabled=false;$('textIn').placeholder=''; updateStatus(); showToast('BLE beacon unpaired','ok'); } function _onBleDisconnect(){ bleConnected=false; document.body.classList.add('ble-locked'); closeSheet(); $('textIn').disabled=true;$('textIn').placeholder='BLE beacon required...'; showToast('BLE beacon lost — locked','err'); addSystemBubble('BLE beacon disconnected. App locked until beacon reconnects.'); updateStatus(); _bleScheduleReconnect(); } async function _bleAttemptReconnect(){ if(bleConnected||!bleGateActive())return; try{ if(!navigator.bluetooth.getDevices)return; const devices=await navigator.bluetooth.getDevices(); const k=getK(); const saved=devices.find(d=>d.id===k.bleDeviceId); if(!saved)return; const server=await saved.gatt.connect(); _bleDevice=saved; bleConnected=true; saved.addEventListener('gattserverdisconnected',_onBleDisconnect); if(_bleReconnectTimer){clearInterval(_bleReconnectTimer);_bleReconnectTimer=null} document.body.classList.remove('ble-locked'); $('textIn').disabled=false;$('textIn').placeholder=''; showToast('BLE beacon reconnected','ok'); addSystemBubble('BLE beacon reconnected. App unlocked.'); updateStatus(); }catch(e){/* retry next tick */} } function _bleScheduleReconnect(){ if(_bleReconnectTimer)return; _bleReconnectTimer=setInterval(_bleAttemptReconnect,3000); } function initBleGate(){ if(!bleSupported()||!bleGateActive())return; document.body.classList.add('ble-locked'); $('textIn').disabled=true;$('textIn').placeholder='BLE beacon required...'; _bleAttemptReconnect(); _bleScheduleReconnect(); } function checkIdle(){ const k=getK(); if(!k.pinRequired||!k.pinHash||chatLocked)return; if(Date.now()-lastActivity>idleTimeoutMs){ chatLocked=true; $('chatScroll').classList.add('locked'); $('textIn').placeholder='Enter password...'; addSystemBubble('Session locked. Type your password to continue.'); } } // ── Video Streaming + PiP ───────────────────────── const activeVideos=new Map(); // id → {video, wrap, title, live, hlsInstance} let pipSourceId=null; function embedVideoStream(url,title,live,muted){ const id='vid-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8); const scroll=$('chatScroll'); const bubble=document.createElement('div'); bubble.className='chat-bubble ai'; const wrap=document.createElement('div'); wrap.className='tc-video-wrap'; wrap.dataset.videoId=id; const video=document.createElement('video'); video.playsinline=true;video.autoplay=true;video.muted=muted; video.setAttribute('playsinline',''); wrap.appendChild(video); // Overlay bar const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='tc-video-bar'; bar.innerHTML=`${live?'Live':''}${esc(title)}`; wrap.appendChild(bar); bubble.appendChild(wrap); bubble.innerHTML+=`
    ${fmtTime()}
    `; scroll.appendChild(bubble); smoothScrollToBottom(scroll,320); const entry={video,wrap,title,live,hlsInstance:null}; activeVideos.set(id,entry); // Load source — HLS or direct if(url.includes('.m3u8')){ loadHLS(video,url,entry); video.play().catch(()=>{}); return; } // Load hls.js dynamically if(window.Hls){ attachHLS(video,url,entry);return; } const s=document.createElement('script'); s.src='https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/hls.js@latest/dist/hls.min.js'; })(); // ── Camera Surveillance + Face Detection ────────── let _survState=null; // {stream, video, canvas, ctx, detector, interval, badge, preview, opts, lastAlert} function makeDraggable(badge,storageKey){ let startX,startY,startLeft,startTop,dragging=false; const saved=localStorage.getItem(storageKey); if(saved){try{const p=JSON.parse(saved);badge.style.left=p.left;badge.style.top=p.top;badge.style.right='auto'}catch{}} function onStart(e){ const t=e.touches?e.touches[0]:e; startX=t.clientX;startY=t.clientY; const r=badge.getBoundingClientRect(); startLeft=r.left;startTop=r.top; dragging=false; } function onMove(e){ const t=e.touches?e.touches[0]:e; const dx=t.clientX-startX,dy=t.clientY-startY; if(Math.abs(dx)+Math.abs(dy)>5)dragging=true; if(dragging){ e.preventDefault(); badge.style.left=(startLeft+dx)+'px'; badge.style.top=(startTop+dy)+'px'; badge.style.right='auto'; } } function onEnd(){ if(dragging)localStorage.setItem(storageKey,JSON.stringify({left:badge.style.left,top:badge.style.top})); } badge.addEventListener('mousedown',onStart); badge.addEventListener('touchstart',onStart,{passive:false}); document.addEventListener('mousemove',onMove); document.addEventListener('touchmove',onMove,{passive:false});     \uD83D\uDC41
    ${typeLabel}

    `); } async function survSnap(){ if(!_survState)return null; const{video,canvas,ctx}=_survState; if(video.readyState<2)return null; await settleCameraExposure(video,140); ctx.drawImage(video,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height); Each frame includes timestamp and camera label (front/back). One-sentence summary of what you see.'; if(prevNarrative){ prompt+=` Previous summary: "${prevNarrative}" — note any changes.`; } // Always use direct Groq vision API — agents can't process images try{ const k=getK(); const groqKey=getGroqKey(k); let responseText=''; if(groqKey){ const visionModel=k.visionModel||'meta-llama/llama-4-maverick-17b-128e-instruct'; const res=await fetch('https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions',{ method:'POST', headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${groqKey}`,'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({ model:visionModel, messages:[ {role:'system',content:'You are a concise visual observer for a photo log. Describe what you see factually.'+TC_CORE_POLICY}, {role:'user',content:[ {type:'text',text:prompt}, {type:'image_url',image_url:{url:collageData}} ]} ], max_tokens:300 }) }); const d=await res.json(); if(d.error)throw new Error(d.error.message||'Vision API error'); responseText=d.choices?.[0]?.message?.content||''; } else { responseText='(No API key configured for vision)'; } if(_plState)_plState.narrative=responseText; // Notify connected agent of the photo log result if(responseText&&k.mode==='agent'){ const agent=getActiveAgent(); const conn=agent?agentConnections.get(agent.id):null; if(conn?.connected){ conn.request('chat.inject',{sessionKey:conn.sessionKey,message:`[Photo Log — Pass ${pass} — ${modeLabel}] ${responseText}`,label:'photo-log'}).catch(()=>{}); } } // Show text-only summary in chat sysBubble(`
    📷
    Photo Log — Pass ${pass}
