    ${esc(responseText)}
    ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}
    `); }catch(e){ sysBubble(`
    Photo Log error: ${esc(e.message)}
    `); } } // ── Watch Mode ───────────────────────────────────── async function startWatchMode(){ if(_wmState)stopWatchMode(); const k=getK(); let facingMode=normalizeFacingMode(k.watchModeCamera||'user');     Watch Alert — ${esc(typeLabel)}

    ${esc(responseText)}

    `); }catch(e){ sysBubble(`

    `); } } async function composeWatchImages(snapshotUrl,refUrls){ // Composite snapshot + refs into one image (for direct mode single-image limit) const allUrls=[snapshotUrl,...refUrls]; const imgs=[]; // stop after claw gone for 0.5s // Claw audio: own mic stream, recorder, chunks let clawMicStream=null,clawRecorder=null,clawChunks=[]; // Live subtitle: show words that fade in then ghost away let liveSubWords=[]; } async function loadMediaPipeHands(){ if(window.Hands)return; await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const s=document.createElement('script'); s.src='https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@mediapipe/hands@0.4.1675469240/hands.min.js'; s.onload=resolve;s.onerror=reject; document.head.appendChild(s); }); } const HAND_CONNECTIONS=[ [0,1],[1,2],[2,3],[3,4], [0,5],[5,6],[6,7],[7,8], [5,9],[9,10],[10,11],[11,12], [9,13],[13,14],[14,15],[15,16], [13,17],[17,18],[18,19],[19,20],[0,17] ]; function drawGestureWireframe(hands){ const canvas=$('gestureCanvas'); if(!canvas)return; const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); if(!rect.width||!rect.height)return; const dpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1; const wantW=Math.round(rect.width*dpr),wantH=Math.round(rect.height*dpr); if(canvas.width!==wantW||canvas.height!==wantH){canvas.width=wantW;canvas.height=wantH} const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d'); if(!ctx)return; ctx.save(); ctx.scale(dpr,dpr); ctx.clearRect(0,0,rect.width,rect.height); // hands = array of {landmarks, color} — empty array or null = clear canvas if(!hands||!hands.length){ctx.restore();return} hands.forEach(({landmarks,color})=>{ if(!landmarks||landmarks.length<21)return; const isRec=gestureRecording; const main=isRec?'#ef4444':color; const secondary=isRec?'rgba(239,68,68,0.6)':(color==='#10b7a9'?'rgba(16,183,169,0.5)':color==='#f59e0b'?'rgba(245,158,11,0.5)':'rgba(125,211,252,0.45)'); const point=isRec?'#fca5a5':(color==='#10b7a9'?'#e6fff8':color==='#f59e0b'?'#fef3c7':'#dbeafe'); ctx.strokeStyle=secondary; ctx.lineWidth=2.5; ctx.lineCap='round'; HAND_CONNECTIONS.forEach(([a,b])=>{ const p1=landmarks[a],p2=landmarks[b]; if(!p1||!p2)return; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo((1-p1.x)*rect.width,p1.y*rect.height); ctx.lineTo((1-p2.x)*rect.width,p2.y*rect.height); ctx.stroke(); }); landmarks.forEach((p,i)=>{ const x=(1-p.x)*rect.width,y=p.y*rect.height; const isTip=(i===4||i===8||i===12||i===16||i===20); ctx.fillStyle=isTip?main:point; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x,y,isTip?4:2.5,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill(); }); }); ctx.restore(); } function classifyGesture(landmarks){ if(!landmarks||landmarks.length<21)return'unknown'; // The Claw: paired fingers with a visible center gap (tuned to be forgiving) const dist=(a,b)=>Math.hypot(landmarks[a].x-landmarks[b].x,landmarks[a].y-landmarks[b].y,landmarks[a].z-landmarks[b].z); const wristDist=(i)=>dist(i,0); const tips=[8,12,16,20]; const pips=[6,10,14,18]; const extended=tips.map((t,i)=>wristDist(t)>wristDist(pips[i])); const [indexUp,middleUp,ringUp,pinkyUp]=extended; const upCount=extended.filter(Boolean).length; if(!indexUp||!middleUp||upCount<3)return'unknown'; const palmSize=dist(0,9); const imDist=dist(8,12); const rpDist=dist(16,20); const mrGap=dist(12,16); const closeThresh=palmSize*0.58; const gapThresh=palmSize*0.22; if(imDist>=closeThresh||rpDist>=closeThresh||mrGap<=gapThresh)return'unknown'; if(mrGap`https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@mediapipe/hands@0.4.1675469240/${f}`}); gestureHands.setOptions({ maxNumHands:2, modelComplexity:0, minDetectionConfidence:conf, minTrackingConfidence:0.5 }); } gestureHands.onResults(results=>{ const multi=results.multiHandLandmarks||[]; if(multi.length>0){ const clawGesture=classifyGesture(multi[0]); if(clawGesture==='the_claw'){ drawGestureWireframe([{landmarks:multi[0],color:'#10b7a9'}]); gestureGoneTime=0; if(!gestureRecording){ if(!clawDetectStart)clawDetectStart=Date.now(); if(Date.now()-clawDetectStart>=CLAW_CONFIRM_MS){ startClawRecording(); clawDetectStart=0; } } } else { drawGestureWireframe([]); clawDetectStart=0; if(gestureRecording){ if(!gestureGoneTime)gestureGoneTime=Date.now(); if(Date.now()-gestureGoneTime>GESTURE_GONE_MS){ stopClawRecording(); gestureGoneTime=0; } } } } else { drawGestureWireframe([]); clawDetectStart=0; if(gestureRecording){ if(!gestureGoneTime)gestureGoneTime=Date.now(); if(Date.now()-gestureGoneTime>GESTURE_GONE_MS){ stopClawRecording(); gestureGoneTime=0; } } } }); gestureCamera=new window.Camera(video,{ onFrame:async()=>{await gestureHands.send({image:video})}, width:320,height:240 }); await gestureCamera.start(); gestureActive=true; showToast('Gesture mode on — show The Claw to speak','ok'); }catch(err){ console.error('[Gesture]',err); showToast('Gesture setup failed: '+err.message,'err'); stopGestureTracking(); } } function stopGestureTracking(){ gestureActive=false; $('clawToggle').classList.remove('on','recording'); $('gesturePip').classList.remove('active'); const gCanvas=$('gestureCanvas'); } // ── Smooth Scroll System (event wiring) ───────────── function easeOutCubic(t){return 1-Math.pow(1-t,3)} function smoothScrollToBottom(el,duration){ if(_scrollRafId){cancelAnimationFrame(_scrollRafId);_scrollRafId=null} const target=el.scrollHeight-el.clientHeight; const start=el.scrollTop; const distance=target-start; if(distance<=0)return; // Reduced motion or short distance: snap instantly if(document.body.classList.contains('reduce-motion')||distance<20){el.scrollTop=target;return} const dur=duration||(distance>800?500:distance>300?380:280); const t0=performance.now(); function step(now){ const elapsed=now-t0; const progress=Math.min(elapsed/dur,1); el.scrollTop=start+distance*easeOutCubic(progress); if(progress<1)_scrollRafId=requestAnimationFrame(step); else _scrollRafId=null; } _scrollRafId=requestAnimationFrame(step); } function snapScrollToBottom(el){ if(_scrollRafId){cancelAnimationFrame(_scrollRafId);_scrollRafId=null} el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight; } function isNearBottom(el,threshold){ return el.scrollHeight-el.scrollTop-el.clientHeight<(threshold||150); } // Touch tracking — know when user is actively touching the chat scroll _chatScroll.addEventListener('touchstart',()=>{_userIsTouching=true},{passive:true}); _chatScroll.addEventListener('touchend',()=>{_userIsTouching=false},{passive:true}); _chatScroll.addEventListener('touchcancel',()=>{_userIsTouching=false},{passive:true}); // Scroll-to-bottom button (wiring) // Only mark _userScrolledUp when user is actively interacting (touch/wheel) _chatScroll.addEventListener('scroll',()=>{ const nearBottom=isNearBottom(_chatScroll,150); if(_userIsTouching&&!nearBottom)_userScrolledUp=true; else if(nearBottom)_userScrolledUp=false; _scrollBtn.classList.toggle('visible',!nearBottom); },{passive:true}); // Mouse wheel also counts as user intent to scroll away _chatScroll.addEventListener('wheel',()=>{ if(!isNearBottom(_chatScroll,150))_userScrolledUp=true; },{passive:true}); _scrollBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{ smoothScrollToBottom(_chatScroll,400); _userScrolledUp=false; _scrollBtn.classList.remove('visible'); }); // ── Keyboard-aware safe area (iOS standalone) ────── if(window.visualViewport){ const vv=window.visualViewport;
